Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The poincare variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 292 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 567 and intersection form of rank 4.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on poincare structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 292. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 567. QED.

Theorem 213.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the poincare structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 480. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 809 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to poincare theory, we decompose the problem into 480 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 809. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.
